# EntropyCut analysis

549 PDF 1.7 figures from 88 complete logs. Methods: EntropyCut and PreSTO-EntropyCut. Every recorded traversal rule and prefetch budget is included; models and common run settings are kept separate.

## Recorded results

- qwen3-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 260.82 → 156.20 s (1.67×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.282% → 3.099%; eviction rate: 39.51% → 89.30%.
- qwen3-8b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 353.53 → 200.22 s (1.77×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.315% → 3.327%; eviction rate: 46.61% → 89.61%.
- qwen3.5-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 412.37 → 203.89 s (2.02×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.265% → 5.299%; eviction rate: 33.15% → 55.96%.
- qwen3.5-9b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 499.48 → 286.96 s (1.74×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.303% → 6.065%; eviction rate: 33.03% → 55.91%.
- qwen3.5-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 8; mean sampler time 39.68 → 30.73 s (1.29×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.349% → 1.659%; eviction rate: 0.00% → 0.00%.
- qwen3.5-9b: fastest recorded configuration is `smallest_cut_diff`, budget 12; mean sampler time 509.44 → 210.42 s (2.42×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.400% → 3.703%; eviction rate: 27.16% → 42.15%.
- gemma-12b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 10; mean sampler time 1101.14 → 469.33 s (2.35×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.806% → 6.065%; eviction rate: 86.42% → 93.10%.
- qwen3-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 283.16 → 163.60 s (1.73×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.259% → 3.134%; eviction rate: 43.90% → 88.36%.
- qwen3-8b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 344.37 → 199.68 s (1.72×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.289% → 3.275%; eviction rate: 44.27% → 89.06%.
- qwen3.5-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 10; mean sampler time 372.99 → 236.35 s (1.58×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.265% → 2.650%; eviction rate: 30.94% → 52.56%.
- qwen3.5-9b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 10; mean sampler time 487.33 → 279.41 s (1.74×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.303% → 2.932%; eviction rate: 34.84% → 53.27%.
- qwen3-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 10; mean sampler time 266.50 → 203.26 s (1.31×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.233% → 1.917%; eviction rate: 40.44% → 88.57%.
- qwen3-8b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 10; mean sampler time 374.82 → 257.40 s (1.46×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.259% → 2.137%; eviction rate: 48.90% → 89.75%.
- qwen3.5-4b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 516.61 → 214.24 s (2.41×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.265% → 5.299%; eviction rate: 32.57% → 55.47%.
- qwen3.5-9b: fastest recorded configuration is `accept_first`, budget 20; mean sampler time 771.28 → 276.26 s (2.79×). Peak occupied KV blocks: 0.303% → 6.065%; eviction rate: 43.68% → 61.04%.

## Measurement definitions and coverage

- Sampler time uses the per-prompt sampler timer, including initial generation and MH sampling. It excludes model startup and answer grading. Logged MH time sums the recorded MH-step durations; these include call preparation and scoring, so they are not pure GPU kernel time.
- Timing and efficiency summaries use every completed sample (5 for 4B; 20 for 9B). Speedup is the ratio of mean times. The paired cumulative-time figures also show sample traces and mean ± one sample standard deviation.
- MH model-call counts exclude initial extension calls. The run-summary CSV separately retains the resource probe's total llm.generate count. Transitions per call is total realized MH transitions divided by total MH calls.
- Cache occupancy is the fraction of the physical KV-block pool occupied, expressed as a percentage. The logs do not provide usable KV-pool GiB measurements, so no KV-cache size in GiB is inferred.
- Exact KV eviction rate is cached physical blocks evicted divided by physical blocks allocated. Prefix hit rate uses hit-token/query-token counts. Prefix miss workload includes unused proposals. Generation memory delta is sampled process GPU memory above the loaded baseline, in decimal MB; recorded zero means the clipped measured delta is zero.
- Engine comparisons plot the original periodic samples against elapsed time from each run's first engine record. Interval eviction measurements marked unavailable remain gaps. Periodic prefix-hit values are retained as logged; aggregate cache comparisons use the resource probe's run-level token counts.
- Call/cut histograms retain all sampled cut draws, including repeated positions. Probabilities are the logged probabilities at sampled positions, without renormalization. Build/generate/score breakdowns retain their logged precision.
- These are comparisons of the supplied runs, not additional independent benchmark repetitions.

## Unavailable diagnostics

- The complete subtree runs used `print_tree=false`. Proposal-level acceptance/certainty and resampling-versus-ratio panels require missing Node records and cannot be recovered. Realized-transition distributions are reconstructed from consecutive MH-call step indices.
